Method: (1) 证明对log-stabilizer fidelity，局域酉优化可化为仅依赖Schmidt谱的闭式解析解；(2) 定义纯化魔力为混合态所有纯化中纯态魔力的最小值，其自由态为归一化稳定子码投影符；(3) 证明纯化魔力可用Uhlmann fidelity给出基于距离的表述；(4) 证明非局域魔力等于约化密度算子酉轨道上纯化魔力的最小值。

Result: 获得了非局域魔力的高效解析刻画，建立了其与混合态纯化魔力的等价关系，并给出了完整的资源理论解释。

Conclusion: 非局域魔力可通过Schmidt谱闭式求解，且与沿约化密度算子酉轨道的纯化魔力最小值精确一致，为量子计算中的魔力资源提供了混合态的理论框架。

Abstract: Non-local magic quantifies the non-stabilizerness of a bipartite quantum state that cannot be removed by local unitary transformations. Despite its natural definition, its evaluation generally requires a difficult optimization over local unitaries. Here, we show that for the log-stabilizer fidelity this optimization admits an exact closed-form analytic solution depending only on the Schmidt spectrum. We then introduce the magic of purification, defined as the minimum pure-state magic over all purifications of a mixed state, and show that it naturally induces a resource theory whose free states are normalized stabilizer-code projectors. For the log-stabilizer fidelity, the magic of purification admits a distance-based formulation in terms of the Uhlmann fidelity. Remarkably, we prove that non-local magic coincides with the minimum magic of purification along the unitary orbit of the reduced density operator. Our results provide both an efficient analytical characterization and a mixed-state resource-theoretic interpretation of non-local magic.

</details>


### [97] [Characterizing Large Scale Quantum Systems with Error Per Circuit Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04132)
*Travis Hurant,Arian Vezvaee,Swarnadeep Majumder,Aniket Dalvi,Jude Alnas,Kenneth R. Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Error Per Circuit Layer (EPCL)，一种基于重叠的电路级量子基准测试方法。EPCL通过对两个不相交的量子寄存器施加相同的随机电路并测量其输出态重叠随电路深度的衰减来估计有效层极化，无需经典模拟理想输出分布或恢复至已知参考态，兼容任意门集（包括非Clifford门）。理论推导、数值模拟和IBM量子硬件实验均验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路级量子基准测试方法存在局限：有的需要结构化门集和昂贵的编译，有的需要经典模拟参考输出，有的需要子系统分解而无法捕捉全寄存器行为。作者希望提出一种能够评估整体寄存器性能、无需经典模拟且兼容任意门集的电路级基准方法。

Method: EPCL方法的核心思想是：将相同的随机电路分别施加到两个不相交的量子寄存器上，测量两个输出态之间的重叠（overlap）作为电路深度的函数，通过拟合重叠衰减曲线来估计有效层极化（effective layer polarization）。理论方面，作者推导了在系综平均去极化模型下的预期重叠衰减，并识别了拟合衰减参数代表有效层极化的假设条件。通过数值模拟研究了弱局部随机噪声、强随机噪声、相干误差以及寄存器间相关性的影响。在IBM量子硬件上对8和16量子比特进行了实验验证。

Result: 数值模拟表明：(1) 在弱局部随机噪声下EPCL能恢复预测的极化；(2) 在较强随机噪声下衰减仍由单指数函数良好描述；(3) 固定纠缠层相关的相干误差可能需要Pauli twirling或随机编译才能产生期望的衰减；(4) 寄存器间相关性会为测量的重叠贡献额外的协方差项。IBM硬件实验在8和16量子比特上展示了清晰的EPCL衰减。

Conclusion: EPCL作为一种无需经典模拟理想电路输出、不受结构化门集限制的聚合寄存器性能测量方法，在理论和实验上均得到支持，可用于评估和比较量子系统的整体性能。

Abstract: Quantum benchmarks provide compact measures of performance that are important for evaluating and comparing quantum systems. Circuit-level benchmarks are particularly valuable because they capture the accumulated effects of noise across interacting operations, but existing approaches may require structured gate sets and costly compilation, classical simulation of reference outputs, or subsystem decompositions that do not capture full-register behavior. We introduce Error Per Circuit Layer (EPCL), an overlap-based circuit-level benchmark that estimates an effective layer polarization by applying identical random circuits to two disjoint quantum registers and measuring the overlap between their output states as a function of circuit depth. EPCL avoids classical simulation of ideal output distributions and recovery to a known reference state, and is compatible with arbitrary gate sets, including non-Clifford gates.
  We derive the expected overlap decay under an ensemble-averaged depolarizing model and identify the assumptions under which the fitted decay parameter represents an effective layer polarization. Numerical simulations show that EPCL recovers the predicted polarization under weak local stochastic noise and remains well described by a single-exponential decay at stronger stochastic noise levels. The simulations further show that coherent errors associated with fixed entangling layers may require Pauli twirling or randomized compiling to produce the expected decay, while inter-register correlations contribute an additional covariance term to the measured overlap. Finally, experiments on IBM quantum hardware demonstrate clear EPCL decay in 8- and 16-qubit implementations. These results support EPCL as a method for measuring aggregate register performance without requiring classical simulation of ideal circuit outputs or restriction to structured gate sets.

</details>


### [98] [Efficient Conversion of Optical to Mechanical States Close to the Single-Quantum Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04150)
*Alexander Rolf Korsch,Liu Chen,Pedro V. Pinho,Boris Müllendorff,Jan N. Kirchhof,Yong Yu,Thiago P. Mayer Alegre,Simon Gröblacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了在毫开尔文温度下运行的腔光机械晶体中，实现接近单量子级别的低噪声光子-声子相干态转换，实现了0.76的记录级转换效率和7.3微秒的机械存储寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的光机械诱导透明（OMIT）转换方案受限于室温运行带来的热机械噪声，只能处理具有大量相干态光子数的经典输入信号。为了实现量子级的声子态工程和混合量子信息处理，需要在接近单量子级别实现低噪声的光子-声子态转换。

Method: 在毫开尔文温度下运行光机械晶体，使用弱相干光输入脉冲（少光子级别）进行光子-声子转换；通过Hanbury Brown-Twiss测量验证转换后声子态的相干特性，量化转换过程中的附加热噪声。

Result: 实现了光子-声子转换效率η=0.76（记录级），机械存储寿命T1=7.3微秒，可调转换带宽超过4.5 MHz；Hanbury Brown-Twiss测量证实了转换声子态的相干性质，附加热噪声nth=9.0。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了光机械晶体作为GHz机械模高效光学接口的地位，为确定性单量子级机械态制备提供了途径，推动了量子信息处理和声子态工程的发展。

Abstract: Coherent interfaces between optical photons and mechanical excitations provide a promising route towards phonon-state engineering and hybrid quantum information processing. Cavity optomechanical systems enable such interfaces via optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT), allowing coherent mapping between traveling optical fields and localized mechanical modes. However, previous implementations of OMIT conversion protocols were limited to classical input signals with large coherent state photon numbers due to room temperature operation and corresponding thermal mechanical noise. Here, we demonstrate efficient low-noise photon-phonon state transfer close to the single-quantum regime in an optomechanical crystal operated at Millikelvin temperatures. Using weak coherent optical input pulses at the few-photon level, we achieve a record-level photon-phonon conversion efficiency of $η=0.76$, a mechanical storage lifetime of $T_\mathrm{1}=7.3~μs$, and a tunable conversion bandwidth exceeding 4.5 MHz. Hanbury Brown-Twiss measurements of the retrieved signal demonstrate the coherent nature of the converted phononic state, evidencing low added thermal noise in the conversion process ($n_\mathrm{th}=9.0$). These results establish optomechanical crystals as efficient optical interfaces to GHz mechanical modes and provide a pathway toward deterministic single-quantum-level mechanical state preparation.

</details>


### [99] [Spurious quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04157)
*Shashaank Khanna,Matthew F. Pusey,Roger Colbeck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子关联与因果结构之间的关系，指出某些在一种因果结构中看似非经典的量子关联，在另一种因果结构中可以有无需微调的自然经典解释（即"虚假量子关联"），但也存在一些因果结构中的非经典量子关联在任何替代因果结构中都无法以无微调的经典方式解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell定理表明量子关联无法在Bell因果结构中经典解释，但替代因果结构（如允许超光速因果影响）可以解释这些关联，只是需要微调。作者希望更系统地理解哪些量子关联是"真正的"非经典（在所有因果结构中都不能经典解释），哪些是"虚假的"（在某个替代因果结构中可以有自然的经典解释）。

Method: 作者分析不同因果结构中量子关联的经典可解释性，对比Bell因果结构与其他替代因果结构。重点探究：（1）哪些因果结构中的非经典量子关联可以在另一种因果结构中获得无需微调的经典解释；（2）哪些因果结构中的非经典量子关联在所有替代因果结构中都无法经典解释。同时考虑相对论对因果结构的自然约束（如空间分离测量不能有因果影响）。

Result: 发现了"虚假量子关联"的存在：有些因果结构允许非经典量子关联，但相同关联在另一因果结构中可在无需微调的情况下获得经典解释，且该替代实现不违反相对论约束。然而，类似Bell因果结构中的非经典量子关联，也存在某些因果结构中的非经典量子关联在任何替代因果结构中都无法通过无微调的经典方式解释。

Conclusion: 量子关联对因果结构的依赖性是微妙的：并非所有"非经典"量子关联都是本质上非经典的，有些只是在特定因果结构下看起来量子而实际上可在其他结构中被经典化。同时存在真正"鲁棒"的非经典量子关联，无法被任何经典因果结构解释，这与Bell场景中的情况类似。

Abstract: In his seminal paper, Bell [Physics Physique Fizika 1, 195 (1964)] considers the correlations that result from space-like separated measurements on a pair of entangled particles. He uses relativity theory to motivate the Bell causal structure, then shows the existence of quantum correlations that cannot be explained classically within this causal structure. Classical explanations of such quantum correlations are possible in alternative causal structures, for instance, those that allow superluminal causal influences, but, as shown in [New Journal of Physics 17 033002 (2015)], all such alternative explanations require fine tuning (causation without correlation). Here we discuss the existence of spurious quantum correlations --- correlations that look quantum in one causal structure, but have a natural classical explanation in another. More precisely, there are causal structures that admit non-classical quantum correlations, but for which the same correlations have a classical explanation in another causal structure without fine tuning. The realisation in the other causal structure can also be achieved without breaking any natural constraints on the causal structure that follow from relativity theory. However, similarly to non-classical quantum correlations in the Bell causal structure, we find other causal structures with non-classical quantum correlations that do not have a classical causal explanation in any alternative causal structure without fine tuning.

</details>


### [100] [Vanilla Exact Synthesis of CNOT Circuits is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04160)
*Chenjian Li,Ji Guan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明"香草式"精确CNOT综合问题（固定数量标记量子比特、无辅助量子比特、全连接CNOT）的判定版本是NP完全的，优化版本是NP困难的。通过将网格图上的哈密顿路径问题多项式归约到超立方体上的哈密顿路径问题，再归约到CNOT综合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前多种相关综合模型已被证明计算困难，但其证明依赖于受限量子比特连接、编码输入或不受限中间变量等额外结构。最基本的设置——固定数目标记量子比特、无辅助比特、全连接拓扑下的精确CNOT综合的复杂度一直是悬而未决的问题。

Method: 证明分两步进行多项式归约：首先通过一元编码映射将网格图等距嵌入超立方体；然后将该超立方体哈密顿路径问题编码为香草CNOT综合问题。主要挑战在于CNOT综合只指定最终奇偶性矩阵，无法直接强制中间顶点访问，为此引入额外的记录量子比特（recorder qubits）将所需的中间顶点访问编码到最终变换中，迫使任何CNOT电路实现都必须体现预期的路径结构。

Result: 证明了香草精确CNOT综合的决策版本是NP完全的（因而优化版本为NP困难），并由此推导出GL(n,2)上的最短词问题、GL(n,2)上Cayley图的距离计算、顺序XOR程序最小化、相位多项式电路精确综合等问题均为NP困难。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了精确CNOT综合最基础设定期复杂度的一个空白，并因其直接推论而表明多个相关代数与综合问题在同样设定下是困难的，为量子电路综合的复杂性理论提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Exact CNOT synthesis asks for a minimum-size CNOT circuit implementing an invertible linear transformation. Although several related synthesis models have been shown to be computationally hard, their hardness proofs rely on additional structure such as restricted qubit connectivity, encoded inputs, or unrestricted intermediate variables. The complexity of the most basic setting---identity input, a fixed number of labelled qubits, no ancillas, and all-to-all CNOT connectivity---had remained unresolved. In this work, we prove that the decision version of this vanilla exact CNOT synthesis problem is NP-complete, and consequently that its optimization version is NP-hard. Our proof gives a polynomial-time reduction from the Hamiltonian-path problem on grid graphs in two steps. First, we isometrically embed the grid graph into a hypercube via a unary encoding map. We then encode this hypercube Hamiltonian path problem into vanilla exact CNOT synthesis. The main challenge is that CNOT synthesis specifies only the final parity matrix and cannot directly enforce the intermediate vertex visits required by a Hamiltonian path. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce extra recorder qubits that encode the required intermediate vertex visits into the final transformation, forcing any CNOT circuit implementation to realize the intended path structure. Beyond CNOT synthesis, our result directly implies hardness for several related problems, including the shortest word problem over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, distance computation on Cayley graphs over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, minimization of sequential XOR programs, and exact synthesis of phase polynomial circuits.

</details>


### [101] [Parameterised graph theory for tensor networks: entanglement rerouting, structural simplification, and agnostic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04165)
*Matthias C. Caro,Natalie McHugh,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用参数化图论研究了张量网络态（TNS）的表示复杂度与学习复杂度，揭示了cutwidth和tree-cutwidth控制TNS转化为MPS/TTN表示的键维度开销，并引入了新的图参数"学习复杂度"来刻画TNS层析的样本和计算复杂度，同时将该框架扩展到非真实（agnostic）学习场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然参数化图论已被用于分析张量网络模拟的复杂性（Markov和Shi, 2008），但其对张量网络表示和层析学习的意义尚不清楚。具体而言，哪些图参数决定一个TNS是否具有可处理的MPS或TTN表示，以及哪些参数控制学习该状态的复杂度，是此前未解决的关键问题。

Method: 论文采用参数化图论方法：首先证明cutwidth和tree-cutwidth限制了将TNS表示为MPS或TTN所需的键维度开销（TTN情形下tree-cutwidth还限制分组子系统的局域维度），证明基于"纠缠重路由"技术（张量网络中经典信息重路由的类比）。其次，通过将(Cramer et al., 2010)的去纠缠MPS学习器扩展到TTN及任意已知图上的张量网络，推导出依赖于图的层析样本与计算复杂度上界。最后，将框架推广到agnostic场景，对任意输入态在加性误差ε内逼近最优张量网络态。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) cutwidth和tree-cutwidth对TNS→MPS/TTN表示的键维度开销给出紧致上界；(2) 基于新图参数"学习复杂度"（可由度和树宽界定）给出realisable TNS层析的图依赖复杂度上界；(3) agnostic学习器对任意输入态能在加性误差ε内达到最优保真度，且具有显式的图依赖复杂度界。

Conclusion: 该工作系统建立了参数化图论与张量网络表示及学习理论之间的深层联系，揭示了图结构参数（cutwidth、tree-cutwidth、学习复杂度）对TNS可表示性和可学习性的决定性作用，并为任意图上的TNS层析提供了统一的、图依赖的复杂度分析框架。

Abstract: Parameterised graph theory studies how the complexity of graph-theoretic problems depends on structural parameters of the input graph. This perspective has proved useful in analysing tensor-network simulation (Markov and Shi, 2008). Its implications for tensor-network representations and tomography are less well understood. In particular, which graph parameters determine whether a tensor-network state (TNS) admits a tractable matrix product state (MPS) or tree tensor network (TTN) representation, and which control the complexity of learning the state?
  We address these questions using parameterised graph theory. First, we show that cutwidth and tree-cutwidth bound the bond dimension overhead required to represent a TNS as an MPS or TTN. In the TTN case, tree-cutwidth also bounds the local dimension of the grouped subsystems. The proofs are based on entanglement rerouting, a tensor-network analogue of rerouting information in a classical network. Second, we derive graph-dependent upper bounds on the sample and computational complexity of realisable TNS tomography, with exponents that depend on cutwidth, tree-cutwidth, and a new graph parameter, learning complexity, which we bound in terms of degree and treewidth. We obtain these results by extending the disentangling MPS learner of (Cramer et al., 2010), as analysed further in (Bakshi et al., 2025; Lin et al., 2025), to TTNs and to tensor networks on arbitrary known graphs. Finally, we extend the framework beyond the realisable setting. For an arbitrary input state, our agnostic learner outputs a pure state whose fidelity is within additive error $ε$ of the optimum over tensor-network states on the given graph with a given bond dimension, with explicit graph-dependent bounds on sample and computational complexity.

</details>


### [102] [Measurements on the separated subsystems of an entangled state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04178)
*Gregory D. Scholes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个解决复合量子系统纠缠态在分离子系统上测量问题的数学框架。作者证明，任何一般纠缠态在分离子系统上测量的投影可以通过考虑张量积空间定义所基于的自由向量空间中的相应状态（陪集）来获得，从而消除了对随机坍缩假设的需要，并从这一视角阐释了非局域性的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠量子系统的粒子性意味着它们可以被物理分离并在分离子系统上分别进行测量。分离系统A和B上的测量应属于各子系统的局部希尔伯特空间中的向量，但一直以来，除了可分离态之外，如何从张量积基下给定的状态中解析出分离子系统的相关状态并不清楚。这一根本性问题阻碍了对纠缠态局域测量的严格理解。

Method: 论文引入自由向量空间的概念，证明分离子系统上测量所探测到的任意一般纠缠态的投影，可以通过考虑张量积空间定义所基于的自由向量空间中的相应（陪集）状态来获得。该方法无需借助随机坍缩假设，从数学上直接构造出分离子系统测量的投影结果。

Result: 结果表明，通过上述自由向量空间中的陪集方法，可以解析出任意纠缠态在分离子系统上的投影测量结果，消除了对随机坍缩机制的需求。非局域性由此被重新解释：它产生于每种分离子系统测量对其可能测量结果进行投影的方式。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个新的数学视角来理解纠缠态的局域测量，避免了随机坍缩假设，并将非局域性归结为分离子系统测量投影方式的结构性结果。

Abstract: The entangled states of composite quantum systems are well studied. The particle-like nature of these systems also means that, while entangled, they can be physically separated and measurements performed on the separated subsystems. Measurements on the separated subsystems A and B should pertain to vectors in the local Hilbert space of each subsystem, but to date it has not been clear how to elucidate the relevant states of the separated subsystems because it is not obvious how to resolve them from states given in the tensor product basis, except for the separable states. Here it is shown that the projections of any general entangled state that are detected by measurements on the separated subsystems can be obtained considering the corresponding (cosets of) states in the free vector space from which the tensor product space is defined. The result eliminates the need to invoke random collapse, and from this perspective nonlocality arises because of the way measurements on each separated subsystem projects possible measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum communication and Bell nonlocality require infinite classical communication to simulate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04182)
*Carlos de Gois,Thyago S. R. Santos,Carlos Vieira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了任意维量子系统中经典通信模拟量子通信及相关性的核心问题，发现从四维（ququart）开始，有限经典通信无法精确模拟量子行为；但在三维（qutrit）情况下，文章构造了使用357个经典比特的精确模拟协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统维度固定但态连续，量子通信与量子关联可以超越经典极限，但又不违反信息传递的限制。此前仅知道最小的非平凡维度（qubit）可用两个经典比特精确模拟。作者旨在解决所有维度下是否有有限经典通信可以精确模拟量子通信与双系统量子关联的问题。

Method: 通过理论分析与构造性协议相结合的方法。首先证明在四维及以上维度，无论共享多少随机性，都不能用有限经典通信精确模拟ququart通信及两个纠缠ququart之间的所有关联（不可能性证明）；然后针对三维qutrit情况，构造一个具体的经典通信协议，该协议使用357个经典比特即可精确模拟qutrit通信以及两个纠缠qutrit间的一切关联。

Result: 发现了从量子维度四开始出现意外的定性转变：有限经典通信无法精确模拟ququart通信及双ququart量子关联（即便允许无限共享随机性）。同时，给出了qutrit情形下的显式协议，仅需357个经典比特即可完成精确模拟。

Conclusion: 量子行为的经典模拟问题上存在维度相关的临界点：qubit（2维）与qutrit（3维）可被有限经典通信精确模拟，而四维及以上则不可能。这出乎意料地表明转变并非出现在人们预期的qutrit维度，而是推迟到四维，揭示了量子维度与经典模拟复杂性之间的深层结构关系。

Abstract: A quantum system of any fixed dimension can be prepared in a continuum of states, yet it cannot be used to transmit an unlimited amount of classical information. Similarly, the correlations observed between measurement outcomes on separate parts of a shared quantum system can be stronger than classical correlations, but they cannot transmit information. These fundamental limitations suggest that the statistics observed from quantum communication and quantum correlations may admit a simulation using a finite amount of classical communication. This expectation is confirmed in the smallest nontrivial quantum dimension, with two classical bits being necessary and sufficient to exactly simulate qubit communication and all correlations between qubits. Despite significant efforts during the previous decades, this remained the only solved case. Here we resolve both problems for every quantum dimension. The solution reveals an unexpected qualitative transition starting at dimension four: no finite amount of classical communication can exactly simulate ququart communication nor all quantum correlations of two entangled ququarts, even with unlimited shared randomness. One might have expected this transition, if it existed, to appear already for qutrits. Instead, we construct an explicit protocol that exactly simulates qutrit communication using $357$ classical bits, and consequently, all correlations of two entangled qutrits.

</details>

### [104] [Universal scaling of fluctuations and correlations across the superfluid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03433)
*Paul Paquiez,Géraud Dupuy,Maxime Allemand,Henri Coquinot,Tommaso Roscilde,Nicolas Dupuis,Adam Rançon,Thomas Chalopin,David Clément*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在超冷晶格玻色气体超流转变附近，实验验证了普适缩放函数的存在——通过数值坍缩不同熵下的序参量累积量提取两个体系特定的标度因子，并证明这些因子可用于统一描述序参量分布和两点关联的缩放函数，同时测定β、γ、ν三个临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变的普适性不仅体现在幂律和临界指数上，还要求所有可观测量遵循普适缩放函数，且微观细节完全由两个体系特定的标度因子表征。此前该预测缺乏系统的实验验证。

Method: 在超冷晶格玻色气体中实现超流相变，通过在不同熵条件下测量序参量累积量并利用数据坍缩提取两个体系特定的标度因子；利用这些因子构造并测量序参量分布与两点关联函数的普适缩放形式。

Result: 成功提取了标度因子，验证了序参量分布和两点关联均遵循由同一组标度因子确定的普适缩放函数，并由此测定了β、γ、ν三个临界指数，证明了多个可观测量在量子多体系统中同时满足普适标度律。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了迄今为止最直接的实验证据，表明普适缩放函数可同时适用于量子多体系统中的多个可观测量，深化了对相变普适性的理解，并为强关联量子模拟器作为普适性标度研究平台提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: The critical behavior of a system near a continuous phase transition is commonly characterized by power laws and a set of universal critical exponents. Yet universality at phase transitions has even more fundamental manifestations. It dictates that all observables follow universal scaling functions, which may extend beyond simple power laws. Furthermore, microscopic details are captured entirely by only two system-specific scale factors, that rescale any measured quantity onto its universal form. In this work, we report the experimental validation of this prediction in ultracold lattice Bose gases undergoing the superfluid transition. We extract the scale factors by collapsing the order-parameter cumulants measured at varying entropies. We show that these factors specify scaling functions governing both the order-parameter distribution and two-point correlations. By measuring these scaling functions, we also determine three critical exponents $β$, $γ$ and $ν$. Our results provide an experimental demonstration that universal scaling is simultaneously obeyed by multiple observables in a quantum many-body system.

</details>


### [105] [Finite-temperature mass gap and quench dynamics of mobile impurities in a Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03656)
*Baihua Gong*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文将从质量间隙描述推广到有限温度，结合Lee-Low-Pines变换和自洽Hartree-Fock解耦研究费米气体中移动杂质的淬火动力学，发现有效质量间隙满足自洽方程Δ(T)=2U_F·tanh[Δ(T)/4k_BT]，在特征温度T*=U_F/2k_B以下存在非零解，并以(T*-T)^(1/2)方式闭合，标志着极化子和分子准粒子热熔化的平均场特征；Ramsey响应中的量子拍振荡恰在T*以上消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于费米气体中移动杂质的质量间隙描述仅局限于零温且未涉及动力学问题，需要将其推广到有限温度并研究淬火动力学，以建立极化子形成的热力学与动力学指纹之间的统一联系。

Method: 将Lee-Low-Pines变换与自洽Hartree-Fock解耦相结合来实现反冲诱导相互作用的处理，并采用泛函行列式方法在该框架内研究淬火动力学。通过推导有限温度下有效质量间隙的自洽方程来刻画系统的准粒子性质。

Result: 得到自洽方程Δ(T)=2U_F·tanh[Δ(T)/4k_BT]，在T*=U_F/(2k_B)以下存在非零解，并以(T*-T)^(1/2)的临界指数闭合；Ramsey响应S(t)的长时间振荡（束缚态与隙内态之间的量子拍）恰在T*以上消失。

Conclusion: 将极化子形成的热力学和动力学指纹统一归结于单个温度相关的平均场参数Δ(T)，该参数在T*处的闭合对应极化子和分子准粒子的热熔化平均场特征及其对应的动力学量子拍消失现象。

Abstract: Recently, a mass-gap description of mobile impurities in a Fermi gas was introduced, which connects Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe for static impurities to the quasiparticle picture of Fermi polarons through a recoil-induced energy gap in the fermionic dispersion. That description, however, was restricted to zero temperature and did not address dynamics. Here we generalize the mass-gap model to finite temperature by combining the Lee--Low--Pines transformation with a self-consistent Hartree--Fock decoupling of the recoil-induced interaction, and we study the quench dynamics within this framework using the functional-determinant approach. At finite temperature the effective mass gap obeys the self-consistency equation $Δ(T)=2U_F\tanh[Δ(T)/4k_B T]$, with $U_F=k_F^2/2M$ and $M$ the impurity mass. This equation admits a nonzero solution below the characteristic temperature $T^*=U_F/(2k_B)$ and closes as $(T^*-T)^{1/2}$. We identify this closing as the mean-field signature of the thermal melting of the polaron and molecule quasiparticles. Computing the Ramsey response $S(t)$ after a sudden quench of the impurity--fermion interaction, we find that its long-time oscillations---quantum beats between the bound and in-gap states---disappear precisely above $T^*$. Our work ties the thermodynamic and dynamical fingerprints of polaron formation to a single temperature-dependent mean-field parameter.

</details>


### [106] [Three-observable measurement of the critical velocity in a paraxial superfluid of light flowing past a mobile impurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03948)
*T. Aladjidi,M. Baker-Rasooli,T. D. Ferreira,A. Bramati,M. Albert,Q. Glorieux,P. -É. Larré*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过双波长光学方案在87Rb热蒸汽中实现二维paraxial光超流与可移动杂质相互作用，利用涡旋计数、流体动力学力和杂质位移速度三种互补观测手段测量临界速度，结果一致且低于Landau判据，验证了paraxial光超流作为研究量子流体动力学中杂质问题的通用平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子流体动力学中，杂质问题（运动杂质通过超流体时的行为）是理解超流临界现象的核心问题。超流光的paraxial体系是一个新兴的实验平台，此前缺乏系统测量临界速度的可靠诊断方法。本文旨在通过多种互补测量手段验证该平台测量临界速度的可靠性和一致性，为深入探索光超流中的杂质问题奠定实验基础。

Method: 采用双波长光学方案在87Rb热蒸汽中构建二维paraxial超流光，其中杂质具有高度排斥势、大且可调的半径和有限质量。通过三种互补可观测量同时监测临界速度：1）杂质尾流中核化的涡旋数量；2）流体对杂质施加的流体动力学力；3）杂质相对于流动的位移速度。将三种方法的测量结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 三种独立测量方法得到的临界速度值相互一致，且系统性地低于Landau判据给出的临界值。临界速度随杂质半径增大而降低，与理论预期定性吻合。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明这些组合诊断方法的可靠性，证明paraxial超流光可作为研究量子流体动力学中杂质问题的多功能实验平台。

Abstract: We report the measurement of the critical velocity of a paraxial superfluid of light flowing past a mobile impurity, realized using a dual-wavelength optical scheme in a warm vapor of $^{87}\text{Rb}$. The superflow is two-dimensional by nature, while the impurity features a highly repulsive potential, a large tunable radius, and a finite mass. The critical velocity is evaluated by simultaneously monitoring three complementary observables: the number of vortices nucleated in the wake of the impurity, the hydrodynamic force exerted on it, and its displacement velocity relative to the flow. These independent methods yield consistent values for the critical velocity, which remain systematically below the Landau criterion and decrease with the impurity radius, in qualitative agreement with theoretical expectations. Our results demonstrate the reliability of these combined diagnostics, establishing paraxial superfluids of light as a versatile platform for exploring the impurity problem in quantum hydrodynamics.

</details>

### [107] [Spectral properties of deterministic matrices multiplied by rotationally invariant random non-Hermitian ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03618)
*Pierre Bousseyroux,Marc Potters*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了非厄米随机矩阵的乘法形变$\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$的谱性质，其中$\mathbf{A}$为确定性矩阵，$\mathbf{B}$为旋转不变随机矩阵。作者证明了当$N\to\infty$时，$\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$的复特征值分布边界由涉及$\mathbf{B}$的$\mathcal{R}_1$和$\mathcal{R}_2$变换的简单方程所决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米随机矩阵在实际应用中广泛出现（如非正规动力系统、开放量子系统等），但其谱分析比厄米情形困难得多。理解确定性矩阵与旋转不变随机矩阵乘积的谱性质，是随机矩阵理论中一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 通过对乘法形变$\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$进行谱分析，借助$\mathcal{R}_1$和$\mathcal{R}_2$变换（自由概率理论中的工具）来刻画复特征值分布的边界行为，推导出在大$N$极限下控制谱边界的简单封闭方程。

Result: 证明了在大矩阵极限下，$\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$的复特征值分布边界由涉及$\mathbf{B}$的$\mathcal{R}_1$和$\mathcal{R}_2$变换的简单方程完全刻画，其中$\mathbf{A}$不必为厄米矩阵。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了确定性乘法形变下非厄米随机矩阵谱边界的精确描述框架，推广了已有理论，并为更一般非厄米随机矩阵模型的分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we study spectral properties of multiplicative deformations of non-Hermitian random matrices. We consider matrices of the form $\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$, where $\mathbf{A}$ is a deterministic $N\times N$ matrix (not necessarily Hermitian) and $\mathbf{B}$ is a rotationally invariant random matrix. We show that, as $N\to\infty$, the boundary of the complex eigenvalue distribution of $\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$ is governed by simple equations involving the $\mathcal{R}_1$ and $\mathcal{R}_2$ transforms of $\mathbf{B}$.

</details>


### [108] [Design Principles for Reproducible Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03852)
*Jasper van der Kolk,Cory Glover,Albert-Lásló Barabási*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出"网络设计"框架，通过编码系统构建模块的局部约束生成设计集，并推导出"单图设计定理"来判断系统能否可重复地组装成唯一结构。研究对3,618个可重复系统进行分类，识别了单图组装与引导组装两种路径，并用3D打印组件进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自然和工程系统（从蛋白质复合物到电子电路）只有在以精确、可重复的方式组装时才能正常运作，但现有网络科学缺乏一致地重现精确拓扑结构的机制，而只关注生成网络系综。

Method: 本文引入网络设计框架，将系统构建模块所遵循的局部约束编码为设计集，并推导单图设计定理以判定这些约束是否保证可重复组装为唯一结构。对于不能唯一确定的系统，提出引导组装作为第二条可重复路径，通过时间排序将构建过程分解为单图步骤。此外，利用3D打印组件进行实验验证。

Result: 将理论应用于3,618个可重复系统（包括蛋白质复合物、分子和机器人），对进行单图组装和需要引导组装的系统进行了分类。识别出多样性-冗余边界，解释了系统如何在保持唯一组装的同时在组件多样性与结构可互换部件之间进行权衡。3D打印实验验证了该理论可将生成性构造集重新设计为能单图组装成预定拓扑的系统。

Conclusion: 网络设计将可重复性重新定义为真实网络可数学检验的属性，为复杂系统的理性工程开辟了新途径。

Abstract: From protein complexes to electronic circuits, many natural and engineered systems function only if assembled in an exact, reproducible fashion. The structure of each of these systems can be understood as a network, yet network science lacks the mechanisms to consistently reproduce exact topologies, focusing instead on generating network ensembles. We introduce the framework of network design where we encode the local constraints obeyed by a system's building blocks in a design set, and derive the Unigraphical Design Theorem, which determines when these constraints guarantee reproducible assembly into a unique structure, a process we call unigraphical assembly. For systems whose design sets do not specify a unique outcome, we identify guided assembly as a second route to reproducibility, in which temporal ordering decomposes construction into unigraphical steps. Applying these results to 3,618 reproducible systems, including protein complexes, molecules, and robots, we classify those that undergo unigraphical assembly and those that require guided assembly. We further identify a diversity-redundancy boundary that explains how systems trade component variety for structurally interchangeable parts while retaining unique assembly. Finally, we experimentally test the theory using 3D-printed components to re-engineer generative construction sets into systems that assemble unigraphically into prescribed topologies. Network design thus reframes reproducibility as a mathematically testable property of real networks, opening a route to the rational engineering of complex systems.

</details>


### [109] [Quenched complexity of marginal states in the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04006)
*Tiziana De Chirico,Luca Leuzzi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型中实现了亚稳（边缘）态计数的淬火计算，首次采用一步复制对称破缺方法，解决了四十年来仅能在退火近似下计算该问题的局限，并发现最低边缘态很可能就是平衡态本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型中，指数众多的亚稳态是边缘态（marginal states），对其进行计数需要破坏BRST超对称性或采用两组复制Ansatz。四十年来，该计数问题仅在退火近似下可解，但退火近似在低自由能区域不稳定，且会预测出低于Parisi平衡自由能的态，这显然是不合理的。因此需要对这一问题进行淬火计算，以获得严格的结果。

Method: 作者采用一步复制对称破缺（one-step replica symmetry breaking）方法，对SK模型中的边缘态计数问题进行淬火计算，替代了此前不稳定的退火近似方法。

Result: 淬火计算得到的曲线在退火曲线失稳处与之分离，并在全复制对称破缺（full-RSB）平衡自由能附近消失，表明最低的边缘态与平衡态相衔接，即最低边缘态很可能正是平衡态本身。

Conclusion: 通过一步复制对称破缺的淬火计算，修正了退火近似的缺陷，并揭示了边缘亚稳态与平衡态之间的深刻联系：最低边缘态即为平衡态，这为理解SK模型及更广泛的自旋玻璃系统中的复杂能量景观提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model the exponentially many metastable states are marginal, so counting them requires breaking the BRST supersymmetry or a two-group replica Ansatz. For four decades this complexity was known only in the annealed approximation, which is unstable at low free energy and predicts states below the Parisi equilibrium free energy. We compute it quenched, with one step of replica symmetry breaking. It leaves the annealed curve where it becomes unstable and vanishes next to the full-RSB equilibrium free energy: the lowest marginal states are thus plausibly the equilibrium states themselves.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Classical dipolar Heisenberg models on the Archimedean and Laves lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02934)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了11种阿基米德平面格子和8种Laves平面格子上经典海森伯自旋通过偶极-偶极相互作用的基态与自旋波性质。研究发现基态分为三类：5种共线、2种非共线（角度与晶格对称性可通约）、8种以超越角倾斜（精确到30位数字）。Luttinger-Tisza强条件的失效与不可通约倾斜的出现完全吻合；是否倾斜由局域配位几何决定，而倾斜角度由长程尾部决定。量子修正由磁基尺寸（通过磁振子谱）主导，与阻挫无关。该结果为dipolar Heisenberg系统在全部1-均匀平面铺砌及其对偶上构建了谱图集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极-偶极相互作用的长程性、各向异性和几何阻挫特性使得其对不同晶格几何上的基态选择、磁序和激发谱产生深刻影响。此前缺乏对阿基米德晶格及其对偶（Laves格子）上偶极海森伯模型系统性的研究，需要全面理解局域几何如何与长程相互作用共同决定磁有序结构和量子涨落行为，并为相关磁性材料和人工纳米磁体阵列提供理论预测。

Method: 对11种阿基米德和8种Laves平面晶格（缩减为15个不同的顶点集）在标准几何配置下，做以下计算：(1) 计算傅里叶空间相互作用矩阵并提取Luttinger-Tisza有序波矢；(2) 在可公度的磁胞上进行无约束最小化确定经典基态；(3) 沿高对称路径评估线性化自旋波色散和Holstein-Primakoff矩缩减；(4) 截断相互作用范围以分离局域几何与长程尾部的影响。

Result: 基态分类：5种共线、2种非共线（可通约角）、8种以超越角倾斜（计算到30位精度）。Luttinger-Tisza强条件失效与不可通约倾斜的出现精确一一对应。截断相互作用表明：是否倾斜由局域配位几何决定（不受截断影响），而倾斜角大小由长程尾部设置。量子修正由磁基大小通过磁振子谱控制，与阻挫不相关——阻挫与涨落分类是独立的；角共享晶格具有最窄的低能分支。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了1-均匀平面铺砌及其对偶的完整族上偶极海森伯系统的谱图集，确立了几何决定"是否倾斜"、长程物理决定"倾斜多少"的两段式机理，并阐明了阻挫与量子涨落的独立性。研究结果为磁性离子占据阿基米德或Laves晶格的材料以及偶极耦合纳米磁体人工阵列的非弹性中子散射提供了预言。

Abstract: We study classical Heisenberg spins interacting through the dipole--dipole interaction on the eleven Archimedean and eight Laves planar lattices at canonical geometry, which reduce to fifteen distinct vertex sets. For each lattice we compute the Fourier-space interaction matrix $\Am(\bk)$, extract the Luttinger--Tisza ordering wave vector $\bk_{0}$, determine the classical ground state by unconstrained minimization on the commensurate magnetic cell, and evaluate the linearized spin-wave dispersion $\varepsilon_{i}(\bk)$ along the high-symmetry path of the (magnetic) Brillouin zone together with the Holstein--Primakoff moment reduction. The ground states fall into three classes: five are collinear, two are non-collinear at angles commensurate with the lattice symmetry, and eight cant at transcendental angles, which we determine to thirty digits. Across the family, failure of the Luttinger--Tisza strong condition coincides exactly with the appearance of incommensurate canting, with no exceptions in either direction. Truncating the interaction range shows that whether a lattice cants is fixed by the local coordination geometry, whereas the value of the canting angle is set by the long-range tail. The quantum corrections are governed by the magnon spectrum through the size of the magnetic basis and are uncorrelated with frustration, so that the frustration and fluctuation classifications are independent; the corner-sharing lattices host the narrowest low-energy branches. The results constitute a spectral atlas of dipolar Heisenberg systems on the full family of $1$-uniform planar tilings and their duals, and provide predictions for inelastic neutron scattering in materials whose magnetic ions occupy Archimedean or Laves lattices, and for artificial arrays of dipolar-coupled nanomagnets.

</details>


### [111] [Dipolar order across Bravais lattice space: classification, spin waves, and a four-attractor phase diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02936)
*Josep Batle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在统一框架下（Ewald求和、Luttinger-Tisza带最小化、线性自旋波与零点修正计算）确定了三维空间中全部十四种Bravais晶格上的经典偶极有序与自旋波谱，并得出三个普适性结论：两个结构原理（偶极张量的无迹性及所有立方对称晶格在k=0处的恒零）解释了有序类型与立方各向异性缺失；面心正交晶格是唯一破坏Luttinger-Tisza构造的晶格；对自由度量参数优化后Bravais空间坍缩为仅四个吸引子，其中体心四方为全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极磁性是三维磁性系统的基础理论问题，而任何晶体都由Bravais晶格加基矢构成，因此系统研究十四种Bravais晶格的偶极有序是建立任何三维偶极磁性系统理论的自然起点。此前缺乏一个统一的、自洽的框架来穷举这些晶格的有序行为，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用统一的三种工具：利用Ewald求和计算相互作用张量；对每个晶格在全第一布里渊区上最小化最低Luttinger-Tisza带以确定经典基态；对所有产生共线有序的晶格计算线性自旋波谱并纳入零点修正。此外，对每个晶格家族进行自由度量参数优化以寻找吸引子，并用超胞直接最小化验证Luttinger-Tisza失效的情形。

Result: 三个非单晶格特有的普适结果：其一，偶极张量在三维中的精确无迹性以及所有立方对称晶格在k=0处张量的恒零这两个结构原理，解释了有序类型、一级立方各向异性的缺失以及零点矩缩减的系统性规律；其二，面心正交晶格是唯一使Luttinger-Tisza构造失效的晶格——其最优本征矢量非圆形，单k态为变长的自旋密度波，LUtting-Tisza给出的能量只是严格下界，需直接超胞最小化获得真实基态；其三，对每个家族优化自由度量参数后，整个Bravais空间坍缩为仅四个吸引子，体心四方为全局最优，菱面体α=62.42°处存在一个低于面心立方的唯一内部最优。三个独立已发表的基准被复现。

Conclusion: 本文系统完成了十四种Bravais晶格的偶极有序统一刻画，揭示了超越个别晶格的结构性原理，识别出Luttinger-Tisza方法的唯一反例（面心正交），并展示了参数空间中Bravais格偶极能量景观坍缩为四个吸引子的深层结构，为研究更复杂的带基矢真实晶体的偶极磁性提供了理论基础和方法框架。

Abstract: Every crystal is a Bravais lattice decorated by a basis, so the dipolar ordering of the fourteen Bravais lattices is the natural starting point for any systematic theory of dipolar magnetism in three dimensions. We determine it here in a single common framework: the interaction tensor is Ewald-summed, the classical ground state is obtained by minimising the lowest Luttinger--Tisza band over the entire Brillouin zone, and the linear spin-wave spectrum with its zero-point corrections is computed for every lattice whose order is collinear. Three results emerge that are not properties of individual lattices but of the landscape. First, two structural principles --- the exact tracelessness of the dipolar tensor in three dimensions, and its identical vanishing at $\mathbf k=0$ for every cubic-symmetric lattice --- explain the ordering type, the absence of first-order cubic anisotropy, and the systematics of the zero-point moment reduction. Second, exactly one lattice defeats the Luttinger--Tisza construction: for face-centred orthorhombic the optimal eigenvector is not circular, the single-$\mathbf k$ state is a spin-density wave of non-constant length, and the tabulated energy is a strict lower bound; direct supercell minimisation gives the true ground state. Third, optimising each family over its free metric parameters collapses the whole of Bravais space onto only \emph{four} attractors, with body-centred tetragonal the global optimum and a single interior optimum at rhombohedral $α=62.42^\circ$ lying below face-centred cubic. Three independent published benchmarks are reproduced.

</details>


### [112] [Decoherence-controlled collective criticality in a two-dimensional quantum Stag Hunt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02957)
*Rajdeep Tah*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文研究了量子退相干对嵌入在方格晶格上的EWL Stag Hunt量子博弈的热力学行为的影响，展示了不同噪声通道如何保留微观策略中性条件但改变系统的热力学相变行为，并揭示了量子纠缠与集体相互作用强度是独立量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解物理退相干如何影响嵌入在复杂系统中的量子博弈行为，特别是微观层面的策略中性条件与宏观热力学机制之间的关系，以及量子效应如何在退相干作用下被经典化映射为统计力学模型。

Method: 将EWL Stag Hunt博弈嵌入方格晶格作为最近邻相互作用，考虑了相位阻尼、去极化及振幅阻尼三种噪声通道；确定了噪声作用下的二人博弈支付矩阵并精确映射到通道依赖的Ising参数；采用Metropolis蒙特卡洛模拟和有限尺寸标度分析；使用资源负性（resource negativity）度量两比特纠缠。

Result: 相位阻尼和去极化共享相同的微观中性分支H=0，但只有去极化以(1-p)^2方式压制相互作用；在β=1时去极化驱动了精确的方格晶格临界点p*≈0.233460，而相位阻尼保持有序共存态；振幅阻尼对通道位置有强依赖且其中性分支在p=1/3处消失；资源负性表明微观两比特纠缠与集体相互作用强度是不同量的；整个扩展晶格仍是普通经典Ising系统。

Conclusion: 量子博弈的微观策略中性条件并不决定其宏观热力学行为，不同退相干通道可以产生截然不同的统计力学结果；量子纠缠作为资源与博弈的战略结构之间的关系需要重新审视；此类模型最终可约化为经典Ising系统进行分析。

Abstract: Physical decoherence can preserve the microscopic strategic neutrality condition of a quantum game while changing the thermodynamic regime of the corresponding interacting population. We demonstrate this for an Eisert--Wilkens--Lewenstein (\textit{EWL}) Stag Hunt embedded as independent nearest neighbor encounters on a square lattice. For the restricted strategies $\mathsf{Q}=i\mathbb{Z}$ and $\mathsf{D}=i\mathbb{Y}$, noisy two-player payoff matrices are determined for phase damping, depolarization, and amplitude damping and mapped exactly to channel dependent Ising parameters $\mathfrak{J}(Γ,p)$ and $\mathfrak{H}(Γ,p)$. Phase damping and depolarization show the clearest contrast: they share the same microscopic neutrality branch $\mathfrak{H}=0$, while only depolarization suppresses the interaction as $(1-p)^2$. At $β=1$, this produces an exact depolarization-driven square-lattice critical point at $p_{*}\approx 0.233460\ldots$, whereas phase damping remains in the ordered coexistence regime along the same neutrality branch. Monte Carlo finite size scaling is consistent with two-dimensional Ising criticality and distinguishes field driven coexistence below $p_{*}$ from a smooth crossover above it. Amplitude damping additionally reveals a strong dependence on channel placement: the post-strategy neutrality branch reaches $Γ=0$ at $p=1/3$ and then disappears. Resource negativity further shows that microscopic two-qubit entanglement and collective interaction strength are distinct quantities. The resulting extended lattice remains an ordinary classical Ising system.

</details>


### [113] [Constituent-Tagged Transfer Linking Cluster Dynamics to Static Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02960)
*Kenji Shinoda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究分裂与合并集群（无唯一谱系）的标度关系，提出通过守恒组分的宿主尺寸追踪定义q*（满足⟨(S_{t+τ}/S_t)^q*⟩=1且q*≠0），并发现q*≈α-2（其中c(s)∝s^{-α}为同一尺寸窗口上的尺寸分布指数），此关系无需拟合即在耦合映射网络、凝并-碎裂动力学和Vicsek系统中成立。半径扫描可定位Vicsek相互作用半径并恢复活性杆实验中独立设定的簇尺寸标度。局部尺寸协方差为该关系提供理论依据，帐篷映射反例则揭示了将每个母体尺寸的稳态平衡简化为单一矩失效的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群合并与分裂过程中不存在唯一谱系（genealogy），这使得追踪演化路径变得具有挑战性。作者旨在寻找一种不依赖谱系的普适标度关系，能够横跨多种动力学系统（耦合映射网络、凝并-碎裂动力学、Vicsek系统等）描述集群尺寸的涨落与稳态分布之间的内在联系。

Method: 作者定义一个标记量q*，满足⟨(S_{t+τ}/S_t)^q*⟩=1（q*≠0），其中S_t为守恒组分所依附的宿主集群尺寸。通过追踪守恒组分的宿主尺寸变化来刻画集群合并/分裂导致的尺寸涨落。在多种系统（耦合映射网络、凝并-碎裂动力学、Vicsek群集系统、实验活性杆）中交叉验证q*与稳态尺寸分布指数α的关系，利用半径扫描进行参数定位，并借助局部尺寸协方差推导理论关系，同时通过帐篷映射反例检验方法的适用边界。

Result: 在所有考察的系统中，q*≈α-2无需任何拟合参数即可成立（在同一尺寸窗口上）。半径扫描方法能够准确恢复Vicsek系统的已知相互作用半径，并能恢复实验活性杆中独立设定的簇尺寸标度。局部尺寸协方差给出了该关系的理论推导。帐篷映射构造的反例表明，当每个母体尺寸下的稳态平衡被过度简化（仅保留一阶矩）时，该关系失效。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种不依赖谱系的普适标度律q*≈α-2，该关系在多个不同类别的集群动力学系统中成立，暗示了合并-分裂过程中局部尺寸协方差与稳态分布之间的深层普适联系。帐篷映射反例则强调了该关系成立需要保留足够多的稳态矩信息，揭示了其适用条件的边界。

Abstract: Clusters that merge and split have no unique genealogy. Tracking the host size of a conserved constituent defines $q_*$ by $\langle(S_{t+τ}/S_t)^{q_*}\rangle=1$, with $q_*\ne0$. Across coupled map networks, coagulation-fragmentation dynamics, and Vicsek systems, $q_*\simeqα-2$ without fitting, where $c(s)\propto s^{-α}$ on the same size window. Radius scans locate the known Vicsek interaction radius and recover an independently specified clustering scale in experimental active rods. Local scale covariance yields the relation, while a tent map counterexample shows when reducing the stationary balance at each parent size to one moment fails.

</details>


### [114] [Sample-specific rectification-like response in a boundary-driven exclusion process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03560)
*Issei Sakai,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究具有淬火位点无序的边界驱动对称排斥过程中的电流响应，发现个体无序实现中会出现类整流行为，但系综平均后电流仍保持反对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无序位点（site disorder）如何影响对称排斥过程的输运性质，特别是从样本特异性空间异质性而非显式方向不对称性出发，理解类整流输运行为的产生机制。

Method: 采用Galerkin投影（线性阶）和平均场闭包（高阶）近似稳态密度分布，推导电流关于储层密度差的非线性函数；分析线性响应对称性、高阶偶数阶电流贡献以及空间反射对称性对类整流行为的影响。

Result: 1）在线性阶，电流响应系数依赖于平均储层密度ρ，与均匀情形不同，导致平衡电流涨落系数在ρ→1-ρ下不对称；2）高阶非零偶数阶电流贡献打破了电流在密度差反转下的反对称性，在个体无序实现中产生类整流行为；3）平衡密度剖面的空间反射对称性排除此类行为；4）对连续分布的无序，类整流行为在近平衡态几乎对所有随机实现发生；5）系综水平上无序平均电流仍保持反对称，因为无序系综在空间反射下不变。

Conclusion: 空间异质性本身（无需显式施加方向不对称）即可在样本层面上诱导类整流输运，但在系综平均层面被无序反射对称性所抵消，揭示了无序系统中输运对称性破缺的微观机制。

Abstract: We investigate the current response of a boundary-driven symmetric exclusion process with quenched site disorder. Hard-core particles hop symmetrically on a one-dimensional lattice with site-dependent rates and are injected and removed at the boundaries by two reservoirs of different densities. We approximate the steady-state density profile using a Galerkin projection at linear order and a mean-field closure at higher orders, and thereby obtain the current as a nonlinear function of the reservoir density difference. At linear order in the reservoir density difference, the current-response coefficient depends on the mean reservoir density $ρ$, in contrast to the homogeneous case. Through the linear-response relation, this dependence leads to an equilibrium current-fluctuation coefficient that is asymmetric under $ρ\rightarrow 1-ρ$. Beyond linear response, nonzero even-order current contributions break the antisymmetry of the current under reversal of the reservoir density difference, producing rectification-like behavior in individual disorder realizations. We further show that spatial-reflection symmetry of the equilibrium density profile rules out such behavior, so broken spatial-reflection symmetry of the profile is a necessary condition for rectification-like behavior. Within the present approximation, we further find that, for continuously distributed site disorder, rectification-like behavior occurs arbitrarily close to equilibrium for almost every disorder realization. At the ensemble level, however, the disorder-averaged current remains antisymmetric because the disorder ensemble is invariant under spatial reflection. These results provide a mechanism for rectification-like transport arising from sample-specific spatial heterogeneity rather than from an explicitly imposed directional asymmetry.

</details>


### [115] [Microscopically exact transport equation for the quantum Calogero model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03018)
*Vir B. Bulchandani,Fabian H. L. Essler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从微观第一性原理出发，推导了量子Calogero模型（直线上一维反平方相互作用玻色子系统）的精确输运方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Calogero模型是研究一维强关联量子系统的重要可积模型，但此前缺乏从微观第一性原理出发的精确输运方程，限制了对其输运性质的理解。

Method: 从量子Calogero模型的微观第一性原理出发进行严格的解析推导，得到其精确输运方程。

Result: 成功推导出量子Calogero模型的精确输运方程，该方程从微观层面准确描述了系统的输运行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子Calogero模型的输运性质提供了严格的第一性原理理论框架，填补了该领域从微观推导精确输运方程的空白。

Abstract: We derive an exact transport equation for the quantum Calogero model, i.e. inverse-square interacting bosons on a line, from microscopic first principles.

</details>


### [116] [Large deviations for linear regressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03111)
*Silvia Bartolucci,Fabio Caccioli,Francesco Caravelli,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用零温复制方法，解析计算了广义可分离正则化线性回归（含Ridge和Lasso）在高斯设计与高斯噪声下、大维度场景中最小训练损失的大偏差统计。所得到的速率函数刻画了最优损失在样本间罕见的涨落行为，数值模拟与理论高度吻合，并显示出尾部区域与典型高斯涨落的显著偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性回归是数据学习中基础而广泛的工具，其最小训练损失（残差平方和的最小值）定义了模型的拟合质量。然而，此前的研究多关注典型涨落行为，对于训练损失在样本间罕见但极端波动（tails）的系统性刻画尚不充分。理解这些极端波动对于评估模型在不同数据集上的鲁棒性和稳定性具有重要意义。

Method: 作者考虑高斯设计矩阵和高斯噪声，教师系数独立采自一般分布p(β)，并针对包括Ridge和Lasso在内的一般可分离正则化函数类，在P个预测变量和N个观测值且r=P/N固定的极限下，利用零温复制方法解析地计算了最小训练损失的大偏差统计，获得了对应的速率函数（rate function）。

Result: 解析计算得到了最小训练损失的大偏差速率函数。大量数值模拟与理论结果极为吻合，并清晰显示在分布的尾部区域，最优训练损失的涨落与典型的高斯涨落存在显著偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维线性回归中训练损失的罕见事件统计提供了完整的解析框架，揭示了大维度极限下训练损失尾部行为的非高斯特征，深化了对正则化线性回归在极端情形下的统计行为的理解。

Abstract: Linear regression is one of the simplest and most widely used tools to learn patterns from data: it fits a set of coefficients so that a linear combination of predictors best matches observed responses. The quality of the fit is measured by the residual sum of squares, the total squared mismatch between predictions and data, whose minimum defines the training loss. We consider Gaussian design and noise, with teacher coefficients independently drawn from a general distribution $p(β)$, and a general class of separable regularizers, including Ridge and Lasso. Using the zero-temperature replica method, we compute analytically the large-deviation statistics of the minimum training loss for large numbers $P$ of predictors and $N$ of observations, with $r=P/N$ fixed. The rate function we compute governs rare sample-to-sample fluctuations of the optimal loss. Extensive numerical simulations are in excellent agreement with our theory and clearly show a pronounced deviation from the Gaussian regime of typical fluctuations in the tails.

</details>


### [117] [Effective Hamiltonian description on monitored Majorana chains: correlated power-law hoppings and unconventional entanglement scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04091)
*Ken Mochizuki,Hisanori Oshima,Ryusuke Hamazaki,Yohei Fuji*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Lyapunov谱分析研究一维Majorana链监控动力学中有效哈密顿量的结构，发现其具有长程幂律跳跃和重要的幅度关联。通过构建随机幂律跳跃模型，证明长程跳跃幅度间的非高斯关联是产生(\ln L)^2纠缠熵标度（而非无关联时的\ln L标度）的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解监控动力学中一个特殊无间隙相的结构，其有限尺寸标度（谱隙闭合快于1/L但慢于1/L^2，纠缠熵按[\ln(L)]^2增长）与常规临界系统或挫折无关系统不同。该标度在传统孤立量子系统的基态中极少出现，因此探究其来源——特别是有效哈密顿量中长程跳跃之间的非高斯关联所起的作用——是核心动机。

Method: 采用Lyapunov谱分析方法求解监控Majorana链的有效哈密顿量。先分析有效哈密顿量的结构，发现其具有随机长程幂律跳跃且存在非平凡的幅度关联（非i.i.d.）。随后构建了捕捉这些关键特征的随机幂律跳跃模型，通过对比有无关联两种情况下的谱隙和纠缠熵标度来隔离跳跃关联的作用。

Result: 构造的随机幂律跳跃模型的谱隙衰减快于1/L但慢于1/L^2，重现了原始系统行为。在无跳跃关联的模型中，基态纠缠熵呈现\ln(L)标度；而在有关联的模型中，纠缠熵被增强，其系统尺寸依赖在被研究的尺寸范围内与[\ln(L)]^2标度一致。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃幅度之间的关联是产生[\ln(L)]^2纠缠标度的根本原因——这种标度在传统孤立量子系统基态中极为罕见。该结果揭示了监控动力学中有效哈密顿量结构的非平凡特性，并指出理解此类系统需超越i.i.d.随机矩阵/随机哈密顿量范式的更精细结构分析。

Abstract: We investigate the structures of effective Hamiltonians governing monitored dynamics of a one-dimensional Majorana chain through the Lyapunov spectral analysis. We focus on a gapless phase characterized by finite-size scalings different from those in conventional critical and/or frustration-free systems; the spectral gap closing faster than $1/L$ but slower than $1/L^2$ and the entanglement entropy growing as $[\ln(L)]^2$ with $L$ being the system size. We find that the corresponding effective Hamiltonians have random long-range power-law hoppings with nontrivial magnitude correlations, rather than being independently and identically distributed. To elucidate the role of these non-Gaussian correlations, we construct random power-law hopping models that capture the essential features of the effective Hamiltonians. The spectral gaps of the constructed models decay faster than $1/L$ but slower than $1/L^2$. We find that, in the absence of hopping correlations, the ground-state entanglement exhibits $\ln(L)$ scaling. In the presence of correlations, by contrast, the entanglement entropy is enhanced and its system-size dependence is consistent with $[\ln(L)]^2$ scaling over the system sizes studied. These results suggest that correlations among long-range hopping magnitudes are responsible for the entanglement scaling that seldom appears in ground states of conventional isolated quantum systems.

</details>


### [118] [Death by mutants: unusual multicritical dynamics in a two-species model for absorbing state transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03123)
*Astik Haldar,Abhik Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个双物种模型（物种A及其突变体B）中非互惠/不对称耦合下的活跃到吸收态相变（AAPT），发现了一个连接物种全局吸收态与均匀活跃态的多临界点。在该多临界点上，临界动力学表现出异常的尺度不变性破缺——物种A在所有维度d<d_c^A下由涨落诱导的对数调制伴随幂律行为控制，而突变体B则属于DP普适类，其上临界维度为d_c^B=4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解物种与其突变体之间非互惠耦合对非平衡相变临界行为的影响，特别是该不对称性如何导致不同物种具有不同的上临界维度，以及多临界点上可能出现的反常临界现象（如尺度不变性破缺）。

Method: 通过构建双物种模型（物种A及突变体B以不对称/非互惠方式耦合），在最低阶微扰理论（场论重整化群/epsilon展开）框架下研究多临界点的临界动力学，分析各物种的临界维度及序参量、关联长度的标度行为。

Result: 不对称耦合导致物种A和B具有不同的上临界维度d_c^A和d_c^B。在d<d_c^A时，多临界点的序参量与关联长度表现出涨落诱导的对数调制下的幂律行为（即尺度不变性反常破缺），而在d_c^A之上恢复常规尺度不变性。突变体B的动力学属于DP普适类，其上临界维度为d_c^B=4。

Conclusion: 非互惠耦合物种间的多临界点存在普适类分层：物种A呈现维度相关的反常幂律标度（对数调制的尺度不变性破缺），而突变体B仍归入标准DP普适类。该结果揭示了不对称耦合对多临界相变普适性的深刻影响，并为理解突变-选择动力学中的临界现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We explore the phases and active-to-absorbing state phase transitions (AAPT) in a two-species model, where the species A and its mutant B are {\em asymmetrically} or {\em nonreciprocally} coupled. We identify a multicritical point that connects the global absorbing state of both the species and its mutant, with a uniform active state. The critical dynamics at this multicritical point is studied within the lowest order perturbation theory. This asymmetric coupling between species A and mutant B leads to unequal and distinct upper critical dimensions $d_c^A$ and $d_c^B$ respectively for A and B dynamics. We show that the multicritical point in this model is characterised by an unusual breakdown of scale-invariance by fluctuation-induced logarithmic modulations with power law behaviour of the order parameter and correlation lengths of the species A at all dimensions $d<d_c^A$. Above $d_c^A$, conventional scale-invariance is restored. The dynamics of the mutant species B belongs to the DP universality class with an upper critical dimension of $d_c^B=4$

</details>


### [119] [Accelerating stochastic processes through nonequilibrium driving: Thermodynamic constraints on the maximum speed-up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03179)
*Davide Santolin,Gianmaria Falasco*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从非线性响应理论出发推导了一组新的不等式，用于约束扰动导致轨迹依赖可观测量存活率加速的上限。在连续时间马尔可夫跳跃过程和过阻尼扩散两种情境下分别得到线性界和指数界，并将这些界与热力学量（熵产生率和过量热）显式关联，最后用单环网络和双稳势阱受迫扩散两个模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有响应理论主要关注系综平均量的变化，而对轨迹依赖可观测量（如存活概率）的加速效应缺乏定量约束。作者希望建立扰动诱导存活率加速的普适上界，并将这些动力学界限与热力学量直接联系起来，从而为随机热力学中的速度-耗散权衡提供新的理论工具。

Method: 作者基于约束路径概率的非线性响应理论（nonlinear response theory of constrained path probabilities）推导界。针对两种随机动力学背景——连续时间马尔可夫跳跃过程和过阻尼扩散过程——分别分析存活概率的变化。对任意含时存活率情形推导出线性界，该界依赖扰动强度及扰动前的熵产生率；对恒定存活率的稀有过程推导出指数界，该界由扰动产生的过量热决定。最后通过单环化学网络和双稳势阱中受迫扩散两个原型模型对不等式进行数值验证。

Result: 得到了两类新的热力学界：其一为线性界，适用于任意含时的存活率，表达为扰动强度与扰动前熵产生率的函数；其二为指数界，适用于恒定存活率的稀有过程，由扰动产生的过量热指数式控制存活率加速的上限。两个模型系统的验证表明不等式在典型非平衡过程中成立且具有可操作性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了轨迹依赖可观测量存活率加速与热力学耗散之间的严格定量关系，为随机过程/随机热力学中扰动诱导的稀有事件加速提供了通用上界，深化了对动力学加速在热力学约束下极限的理解。

Abstract: We derive a new set of bounds controlling the speed-up of the survival rate of trajectory-dependent observables induced by the application of a perturbation. Via the study of survival probabilities in two different contexts, continuous-time Markov jump processes and overdamped diffusions, we obtain different fashions of the bound on the achievable boost. These bounds, obtained by a nonlinear response theory of constrained path probabilities, are explicitly linked to thermodynamics. For arbitrary time-dependent survival rates, we find a linear bound that depends on both the perturbation strength and the entropy production rate prior to the perturbation. For rare processes characterized by a constant survival rate, the bound is exponential and is determined by the excess heat generated by the perturbation. We exemplify the inequalities on two prototypical model systems, namely, a unicyclic network and forced diffusion in a bistable potential.

</details>


### [120] [Generative Nested Sampling of Atomistic Thermodynamic Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03193)
*Alessandro Coretti,Nico Unglert,Sebastian Falkner,Georg K. H. Madsen,Christoph Dellago*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出NS-Flows算法，将流式嵌套采样从引力波推断推广到原子系统。通过对比GW150914似然面与二维Lennard-Jones系统，揭示了原子系统多模态的离散组合性与坐标耦合的密集集体性，并引入以滑动窗口实时集训练的条件归一化流替代MCMC采样。在周期边界LJ盘中能量评估降低两个数量级以上，壁钟时间缩减约三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套采样(NS)是单次模拟解析原子系统热力学的重要方法，但其实际应用受限于马尔可夫链更新在似然约束系综中去相关行走者的瓶颈。虽然基于流的NS已成功用于引力波推断，但将其迁移到原子系统并非简单换一个应用场景——两种系统的能量景观在本质上存在根本差异，需要用流架构有效处理原子系统的独特困难。

Method: 本文首先对GW150914型双黑洞似然面与八粒子二维Lennard-Jones系统进行系统性对比诊断，揭示原子系统多模态的离散组合性(源自硬碰撞壁分隔的粒子置换)与密集集体坐标耦合。基于此诊断，提出NS-Flows：单个以NS能量界为条件的条件归一化流，在滑动窗口的近期活点集上训练，以直接并行抽取代MCMC采样，并通过重要性加权拒绝重采样校正。

Result: 在周期边界条件下的LJ盘系统中，NS-Flows将能量评估次数降低两个数量级以上，壁钟时间缩减约三分之一，且随势能计算成本增长优势更为显著。流的生成效率可作物理诊断工具：沿退火轨迹非单调变化，在密集无序区最低，并由约束系综内部模式复杂度与训练窗口内目标漂移定量刻画，揭示液态系综而非先验-目标分离才是当前流架构的难点。

Conclusion: NS-Flows成功将流式嵌套采样拓展至原子系统，通过活点集自洽供数实现无需结构化先验或预训练数据的流训练。工作不仅显著提升了算法效率，更深化了对不同物理系统中能量景观结构与流架构瓶颈之间关系的理解，指出液态系综是当前流架构的核心挑战领域。

Abstract: Nested sampling (NS) resolves the thermodynamics of an atomistic system from a single simulation, but its practical reach is limited by the Markov-chain updates needed to decorrelate walkers within each likelihood-constrained ensemble. Flow-based NS has removed this bottleneck for gravitational-wave (GW) inference, yet its transfer to atomistic systems is not merely a change of application. Comparing a GW150914-like binary-black-hole likelihood with an eight-particle two-dimensional Lennard-Jones (LJ) system of comparable dimensionality, we show that the two landscapes differ fundamentally: atomistic multimodality is discrete and combinatorial, generated by particle permutations separated by hard collision walls, and its coordinate coupling is dense and collective, whereas the GW posterior exhibits smooth degeneracies and localized parameter coupling. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce NS-Flows: a single conditional normalizing flow, conditioned on the NS energy bound and trained on a sliding window of recent live sets, that replaces MCMC by direct parallel draws corrected by importance-weighted rejection resampling. Live sets supply data self-consistently, allowing flow training without structured priors or a pre-existing dataset. For LJ disks in PBC, the algorithm reduces energy evaluations by over two orders of magnitude and wall-clock time by roughly one third, an advantage that becomes increasingly favorable as the cost of the potential grows. The flow's generation efficiency further acts as a physical diagnostic: it varies non-monotonically along the annealing trajectory, is lowest in the dense disordered regime, and is quantitatively captured by the constrained ensemble's internal mode complexity together with target drift across the training window, identifying liquid-like ensembles, rather than prior-target separation, as the hard case for current flow architectures.

</details>


### [121] [Optimal Control of Periodic Nonequilibrium Mechanochemical Systems via Automatic Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03217)
*W. Callum Wareham,David A. Sivak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用对Fokker-Planck模拟的自动微分，为周期性分子机器（有/无化学状态显式变化）设计了高效的非平衡控制策略，并揭示其设计原则：通过恒速旋转整个角概率分布而不改变其形状来减少机械热，通过减少具有大热量的化学转变比例来减少化学热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子机器是介观系统，需要反复周期性工作以执行细胞任务，同时要应对强涨落和过阻尼环境。最优控制理论可用于理解这些机器高效运转的设计原理，但以往对经典介观系统最优控制的研究大多针对非周期性控制问题。因此需要将最优控制扩展至周期性分子机器场景。

Method: 对Fokker-Planck模拟进行自动微分（automatic differentiation），以设计简单周期性分子机器模型的高效非平衡控制协议。研究涵盖两种情形：化学状态显式变化的机器与无化学状态变化的机器。通过计算梯度来优化控制协议。

Result: 设计出的控制协议和理论分析揭示了这些非平衡系统中高效驱动背后的设计原则：降低机械热的最优策略是让整个角概率分布以恒定角速度旋转而不改变其形状；降低化学热的最优策略是减少具有较大热量释放的化学转变在总体中所占的比例。

Conclusion: 该研究将最优控制方法成功扩展至周期性分子机器，为理解生物分子机器在过阻尼、强涨落环境下高效运转的设计原理提供了新思路，也为人工分子机器的控制设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Biological molecular machines are mesoscopic systems that act repeatedly and periodically to perform important cellular tasks while contending with strong fluctuations and operating in an overdamped regime. Optimal control theory is a tool that can be used to understand the design principles behind efficient operation of these machines; however, most studies on optimal control of classical mesoscopic systems have focused on control problems that do not repeat periodically. Here, we automatically differentiate Fokker-Planck simulations to design efficient nonequilibrium control strategies for simple models of periodic molecular machines with and without explicit changes in the machine's chemical state. The designed protocols and theoretical analysis provide insight into the design principles governing efficient driving in these nonequilibrium systems. Designed control protocols should seek to reduce mechanical heat by rotating the entire angular probability distribution at a constant speed without changing its shape, and should reduce chemical heat by reducing the proportion of chemical transitions with large heat.

</details>


### [122] [Frenkel line of Yukawa fluids within the self-consistent relaxation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03606)
*Ilnaz I. Fairushin,Anatolii V. Mokshin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对Yukawa流体这一简单模型流体，定义了动态交叉（Frenkel线）的条件，并基于自洽弛豫理论确定了rotron极小值消失的热力学状态，所得Frenkel线位置与分子动力学模拟结果一致，并提出了利用静态结构因子直接确定Frenkel线的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frenkel线是液固动态交叉的重要概念，但对其在简单流体（如Yukawa流体）中的精确确定及物理机制仍缺乏统一的认识，需要建立其与微观动力学和结构特性之间的定量联系。

Method: 基于自洽弛豫理论分析Yukawa流体的纵向声学类激发谱中rotron极小值存在的条件，以此作为Frenkel线的判据；通过理论计算确定rotron极小值消失的热力学状态参数，并将结果与分子动力学模拟对比；同时探索利用静态结构因子这一结构特征量直接定位Frenkel线的可行性。

Result: 确定了一组Yukawa流体中Frenkel线对应的热力学状态参数，结果与分子动力学模拟得到的Frenkel线相位图位置一致；证明了Frenkel线可直接由静态结构因子确定；并对Frenkel线附近简单液体中rotron极小值频率给出了物理诠释。

Conclusion: Frenkel线的存在与rotron极小值的出现密切相关，反映了液体中集体振动动力学对粒子运动的支配程度；静态结构因子可以作为确定Frenkel线的简便而可靠的结构判据，同时rotron极小值频率提供了对Frenkel线附近动力学行为的物理理解。

Abstract: For a simple model fluid, the Yukawa fluid, the condition for dynamic crossover, known as the Frenkel line, is defined. The condition is related to the fact that the roton minimum in the dispersion relation of longitudinal acoustic-like excitations exists only when the collective vibrational dynamics of particles dominates in the liquid. Based on the self-consistent relaxation theory for the Yukawa fluid, thermodynamic states are determined in which the roton minimum disappears. The obtained values of the state parameters for the Frenkel line are consistent with the results of studies in which the position of this line on the phase diagram of the Yukawa fluid was determined using molecular dynamics simulations. It is shown that the Frenkel line in this system can be determined directly from the structural characteristic -- the static structure factor. A physical interpretation of the roton minimum frequency for the simple liquids near the Frenkel line is proposed.

</details>


### [123] [Equivalence classes of finite-time transitions in optimal control and non-equilibrium relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03862)
*Jan Meibohm,Samuel Monter,Clemens Bechinger,Sarah A. M. Loos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套随机系统最优控制理论，证明此类问题在临界时刻会发生尖锐的有限时间转变，并依据控制哈密顿量行列式的符号将问题分为抛物型、双曲型和椭圆型三类等价类。作者推导了每类问题的闭环最优协议、成本函数和临界时间，建立了最优控制成本与大偏差率函数之间的映射，并用三组光镊胶体实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机系统在结构化环境（即成本函数含惩罚项）中的最优控制问题普遍存在有限时间转变，但这些转变在实验上难以直接采样（呈指数昂贵）。作者希望建立一种通过普通平均即可访问这些转变的理论框架，从而弥合理论预测与实验验证之间的鸿沟。

Method: 从过阻尼朗之万方程出发，结合二次成本泛函，利用控制哈密顿量行列式符号将所有问题归入抛物型、双曲型和椭圆型三类规范等价类。对每类推导出闭环最优协议、成本函数及临界时刻。随后建立最优控制成本与非平衡弛豫大偏差率函数之间的映射关系，并通过三组光镊胶体实验（平均随机功的控制转变、自由扩散和谐波弛豫中的有限时间动力学相变）进行验证。

Result: 所有控制问题均可归入三个规范等价类；每类的最优协议、成本函数和临界时刻均以闭合形式求得。转变表现出均场水平连续相变的特征。映射关系覆盖抛物型和双曲型两类，而椭圆型不存在简单弛豫对应物。三组实验成功验证了控制转变与有限时间动力学相变的预测。

Conclusion: 最优控制框架为难以直接采样的有限时间动力学相变提供了一条通过普通控制轨迹平均即可访问的新途径，且实验验证支持该理论的有效性。这为结构化环境中随机系统的控制与统计物理之间建立了深层联系。

Abstract: We present a theory for the optimal control of stochastic systems in structured environments, represented by penalty terms in the cost functional. We show that such control problems generically feature sharp finite-time transitions associated with a qualitative change in the control strategy at a critical time. Starting from an overdamped Langevin equation and a quadratic cost functional, we show that all resulting problems fall into three canonical equivalence classes (parabolic, hyperbolic, and elliptic), distinguished by the sign of the determinant of the control Hamiltonian. For each class, we obtain the optimal protocol, the cost function, and the critical time in closed form, and show that the transition exhibits features of a continuous phase transition at mean-field level. We then establish a mapping between the optimal control cost and the large-deviation rate function governing non-equilibrium relaxation after a potential quench. The mapping covers the parabolic and hyperbolic classes, while the elliptic class has no simple relaxation counterpart. This correspondence implies that recently discovered finite-time dynamical phase transitions, which are exponentially costly to sample directly, are accessible through ordinary averages over optimally controlled trajectories. To validate our theoretical findings, we report three experiments with optically trapped colloidal particles: a control transition for the mean stochastic work, and the finite-time dynamical phase transitions in free diffusion and in harmonic relaxation.

</details>


### [124] [Thermodynamic Concentration Inequalities: Controlling Uncertainty in Finite-Time and Small-Sample Thermodynamic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04162)
*Rick Bebon,Aljaž Godec*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了几何遍历扩散过程中广义电流的浓度不等式（非渐近上界），揭示其涨落行为由弛豫时间、局部耗散率和观测量的固有局部涨落共同支配，并突出热平衡态与非平衡态下涨落的定性及定量差异。进一步获得改进的逆热力学不确定性关系，并构建小数据（短轨迹、小样本）下的非渐近置信区间，首次对"轨迹需要多长、样本需要多大"给出定量回答，最后以二维Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程为示例演示如何严格检测细致平衡破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际热力学推断中，人们往往只能获得短轨迹或小样本数据，而现有的浓度界与不确定性关系大多依赖渐近或大样本假设，无法给出有限时间/有限样本下的严格误差控制。作者旨在建立非渐近的理论框架，回答在数据量有限时统计推断的可靠性边界。

Method: 作者针对几何遍历扩散过程，利用其弛豫时间刻画动力学混合速率，结合局部耗散率与观测量固有局部涨落，推导广义电流及其样本均值偏离平稳均值的非渐近上界。方法涵盖马尔可夫过程的浓度不等式工具，并由此导出逆热力学不确定性关系的改进形式（方差上界），进而构造有限时间/有限样本下的置信区间。

Result: 获得了广义电流涨落行为的普适浓度界，其中热平衡态与非平衡态在涨落规模上呈现显著的定性与定量差异；得到了改进的逆热力学不确定性关系；构造了非渐近置信区间，在短轨迹和小样本场景下实现对热力学推断不确定性的精确控制；并通过二维Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程（平衡态与非平衡态）验证了方法能够严格检测细致平衡破缺。

Conclusion: 该研究为有限数据下的随机热力学推断提供了严格的理论基础，填补了非渐近浓度界与逆不确定性关系方面的空白，给出了数据充分性的首个定量判据，为实际应用中基于短轨迹的熵产生估计、细致平衡检测等任务提供了可靠的统计保证。

Abstract: We derive nonasymptotic upper bounds on the probability that a generalized current of a geometrically ergodic diffusion observed for any amount of time, or its sample mean over any arbitrary sample size, deviates from the stationary mean by more than any given amount. The concentration-of-measure behavior of generalized currents is universally governed by the relaxation time of the underlying dynamics, the locally observed dissipation rate, and the intrinsic local fluctuations of the observable. We uncover stark qualitative and quantitative differences in fluctuations in and out of thermodynamic equilibrium. We further obtain refined inverse thermodynamic uncertainty relations, bounding the variance of generalized currents from above. We construct nonasymptotic confidence intervals for controlling uncertainty in thermodynamic inference from small data, i.e., from short trajectories and small samples, and provide the first quantitative answer to when a trajectory is sufficiently long and a sample is sufficiently large. As an illustration, we apply our results to currents observed on a two-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process in and out of equilibrium and show how they can be used to rigorously detect broken detailed balance.

</details>
